    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected

params_input = ConnectionParameters(user='osprey', password='***', database='ospreyflow_xf00a7c3f_gw2', ssl=<ssl.SSLContext obj...postgres'>, server_settings=None, target_session_attrs=<SessionAttribute.any: 'any'>, krbsrvname=None, gsslib='gssapi')

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected

    def setup(self) -> None:
        runner_fixture_id = f"_{self._loop_scope}_scoped_runner"

            self.fixturenames.append(runner_fixture_id)
        # When loop factories are configured, resolve the loop factory
        # fixture early so that a factory variant change cascades cache
        # invalidation before any async fixture checks its cache.
        hook_caller = self.config.hook.pytest_asyncio_loop_factories
        if hook_caller.get_hookimpls():
            _ = self._request.getfixturevalue(_asyncio_loop_factory.__name__)

            )

                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise

            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)

            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)

            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected

        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):

            elif not connected.cancelled():

            raise

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected
E           asyncpg.exceptions.TooManyConnectionsError: sorry, too many clients already

        """Yield again, to finalize."""

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected

    def finalizer() -> None:
        """Yield again, to finalize."""

            try:
                await gen_obj.__anext__()

                pass

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected

params_input = ConnectionParameters(user='osprey', password='***', database='ospreyflow_xf00a7c3f_gw0', ssl=<ssl.SSLContext obj...postgres'>, server_settings=None, target_session_attrs=<SessionAttribute.any: 'any'>, krbsrvname=None, gsslib='gssapi')

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected

config = ConnectionConfiguration(command_timeout=None, statement_cache_size=100, max_cached_statement_lifetime=300, max_cacheable_statement_size=15360)
connection_class = <class 'asyncpg.connection.Connection'>
record_class = <class 'asyncpg.protocol.record.Record'>
params_input = ConnectionParameters(user='osprey', password='***', database='ospreyflow_xf00a7c3f_gw0', ssl=<ssl.SSLContext obj...postgres'>, server_settings=None, target_session_attrs=<SessionAttribute.any: 'any'>, krbsrvname=None, gsslib='gssapi')

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected
E           asyncpg.exceptions.TooManyConnectionsError: sorry, too many clients already

connection_class = <class 'asyncpg.connection.Connection'>

params_input = ConnectionParameters(user='osprey', password='***', database='ospreyflow_xf00a7c3f_gw3', ssl=<ssl.SSLContext obj...postgres'>, server_settings=None, target_session_attrs=<SessionAttribute.any: 'any'>, krbsrvname=None, gsslib='gssapi')

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected
E           asyncpg.exceptions.TooManyConnectionsError: sorry, too many clients already

connection_class = <class 'asyncpg.connection.Connection'>

params_input = ConnectionParameters(user='osprey', password='***', database='ospreyflow_xf00a7c3f_gw3', ssl=<ssl.SSLContext obj...postgres'>, server_settings=None, target_session_attrs=<SessionAttribute.any: 'any'>, krbsrvname=None, gsslib='gssapi')

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected
E           asyncpg.exceptions.TooManyConnectionsError: sorry, too many clients already

connection_class = <class 'asyncpg.connection.Connection'>
record_class = <class 'asyncpg.protocol.record.Record'>
params_input = ConnectionParameters(user='osprey', password='***', database='ospreyflow_xf00a7c3f_gw0', ssl=<ssl.SSLContext obj...postgres'>, server_settings=None, target_session_attrs=<SessionAttribute.any: 'any'>, krbsrvname=None, gsslib='gssapi')

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected
E           asyncpg.exceptions.TooManyConnectionsError: sorry, too many clients already

params_input = ConnectionParameters(user='osprey', password='***', database='ospreyflow_xf00a7c3f_gw2', ssl=<ssl.SSLContext obj...postgres'>, server_settings=None, target_session_attrs=<SessionAttribute.any: 'any'>, krbsrvname=None, gsslib='gssapi')

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected
E           asyncpg.exceptions.TooManyConnectionsError: sorry, too many clients already

        conn = await asyncpg.connect(dsn=PostgresConfig.from_env().dsn)
        await conn.execute(f'CREATE SCHEMA "{name}"')
        await conn.close()
        yield name

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected
E           asyncpg.exceptions.TooManyConnectionsError: sorry, too many clients already

            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected

params_input = ConnectionParameters(user='osprey', password='***', database='ospreyflow_xf00a7c3f_gw2', ssl=<ssl.SSLContext obj...'search_path': 'acc_734775238d'}, target_session_attrs=<SessionAttribute.any: 'any'>, krbsrvname=None, gsslib='gssapi')

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected
E           asyncpg.exceptions.TooManyConnectionsError: sorry, too many clients already

                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise

>           await connected

params_input = ConnectionParameters(user='osprey', password='***', database='ospreyflow_xf00a7c3f_gw3', ssl=<ssl.SSLContext obj...'search_path': 'acc_2b094aeb84'}, target_session_attrs=<SessionAttribute.any: 'any'>, krbsrvname=None, gsslib='gssapi')

    async def __connect_addr(
        params,
        retry,
        addr,
        loop,
        config,
        connection_class,
        record_class,
        params_input,
    ):
        connected = _create_future(loop)
    
        proto_factory = lambda: protocol.Protocol(
            addr, connected, params, record_class, loop)
    
        if isinstance(addr, str):
            # UNIX socket
            connector = loop.create_unix_connection(proto_factory, addr)
    
        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected
E           asyncpg.exceptions.TooManyConnectionsError: sorry, too many clients already

        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )
    
        elif params.ssl:
            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
    
        try:
>           await connected

                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)

                case_id = made.json()["id"]
                cases.append(case_id)
                _run(_give_case(case_id))
    
                # The ruling of 9 November 2026: the case's own searches match a gazetteer place on its
                # name alone. The exact search by its `osm_id` finds nothing; the type-ahead on the
                # identifier's prefix suggests nothing and says no `more`, though 12 places share it.
                by_id = client.post(
                    f"/v1/investigations/{case_id}/search", headers=headers, json={"query": OSM_ID}
                )
                assert by_id.status_code == 200 and by_id.json()["nodes"] == [], by_id.text
                for body in (
                    {"query": OSM_ID},
                    {"query": OSM_ID, "mode": "contains", "ignore_case": True},
                ):
                    hinted = client.post(
                        f"/v1/investigations/{case_id}/suggest", headers=headers, json=body
                    )
                    assert hinted.status_code == 200, hinted.text
                    assert hinted.json()["nodes"] == [] and hinted.json()["more"] is False, body
                found = client.post(
                    f"/v1/investigations/{case_id}/search", headers=headers, json={"query": NAME}
                )
                assert found.status_code == 200, found.text
                assert [n["id"] for n in found.json()["nodes"]] == [place_id]
                assert found.json()["nodes"][0]["properties"]["name"] == NAME
                assert not _carries_an_identifier(found.text), found.text
    
                seeded = client.post(
                    f"/v1/investigations/{case_id}/seed",
                    headers=headers,
                    json={"nodes": [found.json()["nodes"][0]], "edges": []},
                )
                assert seeded.status_code == 200, seeded.text
                assert not _carries_an_identifier(seeded.text), seeded.text
    
                # A second case, from the person alone, so the place arrives by expansion.
                other = client.post("/v1/investigations", headers=headers, json=new_case())
                other_id = other.json()["id"]
                cases.append(other_id)
                _run(_give_case(other_id))
                put = client.post(
                    f"/v1/investigations/{other_id}/seed",
                    headers=headers,
                    json={"nodes": [person_body], "edges": []},
                )
                assert put.status_code == 200, put.text
                expanded = client.post(
                    f"/v1/investigations/{other_id}/expand",
                    headers=headers,
                    json={"request": {"node_ids": [neighbour_id], "depth": 1}},
                )
                assert expanded.status_code == 200, expanded.text
                assert place_id in {n["id"] for n in expanded.json()["delta"]["nodes"]}, expanded.text

        elif params.ssl and params.ssl_negotiation is SSLNegotiation.direct:
            # if ssl and ssl_negotiation is `direct`, skip STARTTLS and perform
            # direct SSL connection
            connector = loop.create_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, ssl=params.ssl
            )

            connector = _create_ssl_connection(
                proto_factory, *addr, loop=loop, ssl_context=params.ssl,
                ssl_is_advisory=params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer,
                retry=retry and params.sslmode == SSLMode.prefer)
        else:
            connector = loop.create_connection(proto_factory, *addr)
    
        try:
            tr, pr = await connector
        except (Exception, asyncio.CancelledError):
            # The protocol can exist before create_connection() returns.  If
            # that operation is cancelled, nobody will await authentication.
            if not connected.done():
                connected.cancel()
            elif not connected.cancelled():
                connected.exception()
            raise
